::: iframe

::: {.mw-content-ltr .mw-parser-output lang="en" dir="ltr"}
***Battle Angel Alita: Last Order***, known in Japan as ***Gunnm: Last
Order***[ ([銃夢 Last Order]{.t_nihongo_kanji
lang="ja"}[,]{.t_nihongo_comma style="display:none"} *[Ganmu Rasuto
Ōdā]{.t_nihongo_romaji}*[^[[?]{.t_nihongo_icon
style="color:#00e;font:bold 80% sans-serif;text-decoration:none;padding:0 .1em;"}](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Help:Japanese "wikipedia:Help:Japanese"){.extiw}^]{.t_nihongo_help})]{style="font-weight: normal"},
is a manga series by [Yukito
Kishiro](/wiki/Yukito_Kishiro "Yukito Kishiro") that serves as the
sequel to *[Battle Angel
Alita](/wiki/Battle_Angel_Alita "Battle Angel Alita")*. It continues the
story of [Alita](/wiki/Alita "Alita"); continuing her quest to uncover
her mysterious past, expanding beyond [Earth]{.new
title="Earth (page does not exist)"
uncrawlable-url="L3dpa2kvRWFydGg/YWN0aW9uPWVkaXQmcmVkbGluaz0x"} to occur
primarily in space. The manga ran from November 2000 to January 2014,
making it Kishiro\'s longest running manga. It was followed in October
2014 by the direct sequel *[Battle Angel Alita: Mars
Chronicle](/wiki/Battle_Angel_Alita:_Mars_Chronicle "Battle Angel Alita: Mars Chronicle")*.

::: {#toc .toc role="navigation" aria-labelledby="mw-toc-heading"}
::: {.toctitle lang="en" dir="ltr"}

        [Internationalization]{.toctext}](#Internationalization)
-   [[5]{.tocnumber} [References]{.toctext}](#References)

:::

## [Plot]{#Plot .mw-headline}[[\[]{.mw-editsection-bracket}[![](data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2ZyBjbGFzcz0id2RzLWljb24gd2RzLWljb24tdGlueSIgYXJpYS1oaWRkZW49InRydWUiIGZvY3VzYWJsZT0iZmFsc2UiPjx1c2UgeGxpbms6aHJlZj0iI3dkcy1pY29ucy1wZW5jaWwtdGlueSI+PC91c2U+PC9zdmc+){.wds-icon .wds-icon-tiny}](https://battleangel.fandom.com/wiki/Battle_Angel_Alita:_Last_Order?veaction=edit&section=1 "Edit section: Plot"){.mw-editsection-visualeditor tracking-label="edit-section-anon" data-action="edit-section" aria-label="Edit section: Plot"}[\]]{.mw-editsection-bracket}]{.mw-editsection}

*Last Order* continues from [the final volume]{.new
title="Angel's Ascension (page does not exist)"
uncrawlable-url="L3dpa2kvQW5nZWwlMjdzX0FzY2Vuc2lvbj9hY3Rpb249ZWRpdCZyZWRsaW5rPTE="}
of *[Battle Angel
Alita](/wiki/Battle_Angel_Alita "Battle Angel Alita")*, but diverges
from the [original
ending](/wiki/Battle_Angel_Alita_Epilogue "Battle Angel Alita Epilogue").
It ignores the transformation of [Ketheres](/wiki/Ketheres "Ketheres")
into a [nanotechnological space flower]{.new
title="Life-Tree (page does not exist)"
uncrawlable-url="L3dpa2kvTGlmZS1UcmVlP2FjdGlvbj1lZGl0JnJlZGxpbms9MQ=="},
[Alita](/wiki/Alita "Alita")\'s subsequent transformation into a flesh
and blood [human](/wiki/Human "Human"), and her reunion with [Figure
Four](/wiki/Figure_Four "Figure Four"). Instead, it takes place after
Alita was killed by a [doll bomb](/wiki/Doll_bomb "Doll bomb") planted
by [Desty Nova](/wiki/Desty_Nova "Desty Nova").

The story begins when Alita is resurrected by Nova\'s
[nanotechnology]{.new title="Nanotechnology (page does not exist)"
uncrawlable-url="L3dpa2kvTmFub3RlY2hub2xvZ3k/YWN0aW9uPWVkaXQmcmVkbGluaz0x"}
in the floating city of
[Tiphares](/wiki/Tiphares "Tiphares"){.mw-redirect} a year later. The
[secret of
Tiphares](/wiki/Secret_of_Tiphares "Secret of Tiphares"){.mw-redirect}
is publicly exposed and results in a [brutal civil
war](/wiki/Tipharean_civil_war "Tipharean civil war"), but Tiphares and
the surface turn out to be a small part of a [more complex
world](/wiki/World_of_Battle_Angel_Alita "World of Battle Angel Alita").
Going into space with new and old companions alike to rescue her lost
friend [Lou Collins](/wiki/Lou_Collins "Lou Collins") and find out more
about her forgotten past, Alita is caught up in an interplanetary
struggle between the major powers of the colonized [Solar
System](/wiki/Solar_System "Solar System"). Along the way, she forms an
alliance with three of the former [TUNED](/wiki/TUNED "TUNED") agents
who have now begun to think for themselves, an [unsavory
superhacker](/wiki/Ping_Wu "Ping Wu"), and Nova himself when she enters
the [LADDER](/wiki/LADDER "LADDER")-sponsored [Tenth Zenith of Things
Tournament](/wiki/Tenth_Zenith_of_Things_Tournament "Tenth Zenith of Things Tournament")
(ZOTT), a [Solar System](/wiki/Solar_System "Solar System")-wide
fighting tournament held every ten years. During the course of the
story, more background about the setting of *Battle Angel Alita* that
was not disclosed in the prior series is revealed, such as how the
[Earth]{.new title="Earth (page does not exist)"
uncrawlable-url="L3dpa2kvRWFydGg/YWN0aW9uPWVkaXQmcmVkbGluaz0x"} emerged
from a cataclysmic impact winter that wiped out most of
[humanity](/wiki/Human "Human").

The ZOTT, which takes place over the course of several days, eventually
forms the primary story arc of *Last Order* as the motivations and
backgrounds of additional teams are revealed while other events
transpire in the background. With Alita\'s [Space
Angels](/wiki/Space_Angels "Space Angels") making it to the Finals and
eventually triumphing, the stage is set for her to again affect the
course of human history.

## [Background]{#Background .mw-headline}[[\[]{.mw-editsection-bracket}[![](data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2ZyBjbGFzcz0id2RzLWljb24gd2RzLWljb24tdGlueSIgYXJpYS1oaWRkZW49InRydWUiIGZvY3VzYWJsZT0iZmFsc2UiPjx1c2UgeGxpbms6aHJlZj0iI3dkcy1pY29ucy1wZW5jaWwtdGlueSI+PC91c2U+PC9zdmc+){.wds-icon .wds-icon-tiny}](https://battleangel.fandom.com/wiki/Battle_Angel_Alita:_Last_Order?veaction=edit&section=2 "Edit section: Background"){.mw-editsection-visualeditor tracking-label="edit-section-anon" data-action="edit-section" aria-label="Edit section: Background"}[\]]{.mw-editsection-bracket}]{.mw-editsection}

[Yukito Kishiro](/wiki/Yukito_Kishiro "Yukito Kishiro") has stated that
he was sick at the time of *Battle Angel Alita* and was forced to cut it
short with an ending he was not satisfied with. After working on other
projects and the manga *[Aqua Knight](/wiki/Aqua_Knight "Aqua Knight")*,
he decided to go back to Alita\'s story in 2000. Originally, the story
was planned to follow that of the PlayStation game *[Gunnm: Martian
Memory](/wiki/Gunnm:_Martian_Memory "Gunnm: Martian Memory")*, but its
story is only used partially and sparingly, as Kishiro expanded and
changed the
story.^[[\[]{.cite-bracket}1[\]]{.cite-bracket}](#cite_note-1)^

## [Reader submissions]{#Reader_submissions .mw-headline}[[\[]{.mw-editsection-bracket}[![](data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2ZyBjbGFzcz0id2RzLWljb24gd2RzLWljb24tdGlueSIgYXJpYS1oaWRkZW49InRydWUiIGZvY3VzYWJsZT0iZmFsc2UiPjx1c2UgeGxpbms6aHJlZj0iI3dkcy1pY29ucy1wZW5jaWwtdGlueSI+PC91c2U+PC9zdmc+){.wds-icon .wds-icon-tiny}](https://battleangel.fandom.com/wiki/Battle_Angel_Alita:_Last_Order?veaction=edit&section=3 "Edit section: Reader submissions"){.mw-editsection-visualeditor tracking-label="edit-section-anon" data-action="edit-section" aria-label="Edit section: Reader submissions"}[\]]{.mw-editsection-bracket}]{.mw-editsection}

Kishiro has solicited his readers for suggestions on character designs
for *Last Order*, which can be submitted through his website,
[Yukitopia](/wiki/Yukitopia "Yukitopia"). If accepted, he retains the
copyright for the adaptation. Accepted designs are highlighted at the
end of the volumes and in a special section of Yukitopia. The most
notable characters that were based on reader submissions are [Aga
Mbadi](/wiki/Aga_Mbadi "Aga Mbadi") and [Caerula
Sanguis](/wiki/Caerula_Sanguis "Caerula Sanguis").

## [Publication]{#Publication .mw-headline}[[\[]{.mw-editsection-bracket}[![](data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2ZyBjbGFzcz0id2RzLWljb24gd2RzLWljb24tdGlueSIgYXJpYS1oaWRkZW49InRydWUiIGZvY3VzYWJsZT0iZmFsc2UiPjx1c2UgeGxpbms6aHJlZj0iI3dkcy1pY29ucy1wZW5jaWwtdGlueSI+PC91c2U+PC9zdmc+){.wds-icon .wds-icon-tiny}](https://battleangel.fandom.com/wiki/Battle_Angel_Alita:_Last_Order?veaction=edit&section=4 "Edit section: Publication"){.mw-editsection-visualeditor tracking-label="edit-section-anon" data-action="edit-section" aria-label="Edit section: Publication"}[\]]{.mw-editsection-bracket}]{.mw-editsection}

*Last Order* was first published by
[Shueisha](/wiki/Shueisha "Shueisha"), Kishiro\'s longtime publisher,
beginning in July 2001 and serialized in *[Ultra
Jump](/wiki/Ultra_Jump "Ultra Jump")*. In June 2010 it went on hiatus
since the [100th installment](/wiki/Phase_100 "Phase 100") due to a
disagreement between Kishiro and an editor at *Ultra Jump*. The editor
had wanted Kishiro to refrain from using the word \"psycho\" in his
manga dialogue for the new reprint of *Battle Angel Alita*, the *[Gunnm:
New
Edition](/wiki/Gunnm:_New_Edition "Gunnm: New Edition")*.^[[\[]{.cite-bracket}2[\]]{.cite-bracket}](#cite_note-2)^
Kishiro later offered to resume the manga only if the legal department
of Ultra Jump apologized to him for questioning the three sections of
dialogue in the *New Edition* and Shueisha would recall it and revert
the revised sections to their original form. If his requirements were
not met, Kishiro threatened to switch publishers to
[Kodansha](/wiki/Kodansha "Kodansha")\'s
*[Evening](/wiki/Evening "Evening")*
magazine.^[[\[]{.cite-bracket}3[\]]{.cite-bracket}](#cite_note-3)^
Kishiro\'s requirements were apparently rejected by Shueisha and
Kodansha subsequently announced that *Last Order* would join Evening in
the eighth issue in March
2011^[[\[]{.cite-bracket}4[\]]{.cite-bracket}](#cite_note-4)^, which was
released on March 22 with [Phase 101](/wiki/Phase_101 "Phase 101") of
the manga.^[[\[]{.cite-bracket}5[\]]{.cite-bracket}](#cite_note-5)^

The first 15 volumes of the tankōbon were published by Shueisha, and
Kodansha has continued with the 16th volume. Kodansha also began
publication of the *[Gunnm: Last Order New
Edition](/wiki/Gunnm:_Last_Order_New_Edition "Gunnm: Last Order New Edition")*,
a reprint of *Last Order*, in July
2011.^[[\[]{.cite-bracket}6[\]]{.cite-bracket}](#cite_note-6)^

### [Internationalization]{#Internationalization .mw-headline}[[\[]{.mw-editsection-bracket}[![](data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2ZyBjbGFzcz0id2RzLWljb24gd2RzLWljb24tdGlueSIgYXJpYS1oaWRkZW49InRydWUiIGZvY3VzYWJsZT0iZmFsc2UiPjx1c2UgeGxpbms6aHJlZj0iI3dkcy1pY29ucy1wZW5jaWwtdGlueSI+PC91c2U+PC9zdmc+){.wds-icon .wds-icon-tiny}](https://battleangel.fandom.com/wiki/Battle_Angel_Alita:_Last_Order?veaction=edit&section=5 "Edit section: Internationalization"){.mw-editsection-visualeditor tracking-label="edit-section-anon" data-action="edit-section" aria-label="Edit section: Internationalization"}[\]]{.mw-editsection-bracket}]{.mw-editsection}

The manga is published internationally in the following languages:

-   Chinese: By [Tong Li Comics]{.new
    title="Tong Li Comics (page does not exist)"
    uncrawlable-url="L3dpa2kvVG9uZ19MaV9Db21pY3M/YWN0aW9uPWVkaXQmcmVkbGluaz0x"}
    as 銃夢II Last Order[ ([Gun Dream II Last Order]{.t_nihongo_kanji
    lang="ja"}[[,]{.t_nihongo_comma style="display:none"} *[銃夢II Last
    Order]{.t_nihongo_romaji}*]{.t_nihongo_norom
    style="display:none"}[^[[?]{.t_nihongo_icon
    style="color:#00e;font:bold 80% sans-serif;text-decoration:none;padding:0 .1em;"}](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Help:Japanese "wikipedia:Help:Japanese"){.extiw}^]{.t_nihongo_help})]{style="font-weight: normal"}.
-   English: By [Viz Media](/wiki/Viz_Media "Viz Media") as *Battle
    Angel Alita: Last Order*. Because of the switch in publishers to
    Kodansha, its American distributor, [Kodansha Comics USA]{.new
    title="Kodansha Comics USA (page does not exist)"
    uncrawlable-url="L3dpa2kvS29kYW5zaGFfQ29taWNzX1VTQT9hY3Rpb249ZWRpdCZyZWRsaW5rPTE="},
    replaced Viz as the current publisher starting with [Volume 16]{.new
    title="Volume 16 (page does not exist)"
    uncrawlable-url="L3dpa2kvVm9sdW1lXzE2P2FjdGlvbj1lZGl0JnJlZGxpbms9MQ=="}.
-   French: By [Glénat Manga]{.new
    title="Glénat Manga (page does not exist)"
    uncrawlable-url="L3dpa2kvR2wlQzMlQTluYXRfTWFuZ2E/YWN0aW9uPWVkaXQmcmVkbGluaz0x"}
    as *Gunnm Last Order*.
-   German: By [Carlsen Comics]{.new
    title="Carlsen Comics (page does not exist)"
    uncrawlable-url="L3dpa2kvQ2FybHNlbl9Db21pY3M/YWN0aW9uPWVkaXQmcmVkbGluaz0x"}
    as *Battle Angel Alita - Last Order*.
-   Spanish: By [Planeta DeAgostini Comics]{.new
    title="Planeta DeAgostini Comics (page does not exist)"
    uncrawlable-url="L3dpa2kvUGxhbmV0YV9EZUFnb3N0aW5pX0NvbWljcz9hY3Rpb249ZWRpdCZyZWRsaW5rPTE="}
    as *Gunnm Last Order*. The number of volumes is higher as they do
    not correspond exactly to the original tankōbon.
-   Italian: By Panini Comics as Alita Last Order. It\'s published into
    two versions, an (old) 38 volumes B6 size, and a (new) \"Deluxe\"
    version A5 size.
